<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زُرَيْقِ بْنِ حَكِيمٍ الأَيْلِيِّ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، يُقَالُ لَهُ مِصْبَاحٌ اسْتَعَانَ ابْنًا لَهُ فَكَأَنَّهُ اسْتَبْطَأَهُ فَلَمَّا جَاءَهُ قَالَ لَهُ يَا زَانٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ زُرَيْقٌ فَاسْتَعْدَانِي عَلَيْهِ فَلَمَّا أَرَدْتُ أَنْ أَجْلِدَهُ قَالَ ابْنُهُ وَاللَّهِ لَئِنْ جَلَدْتَهُ لأَبُوأَنَّ عَلَى نَفْسِي بِالزِّنَا ‏.‏ فَلَمَّا قَالَ ذَلِكَ أَشْكَلَ عَلَىَّ أَمْرُهُ فَكَتَبْتُ فِيهِ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ - وَهُوَ الْوَالِي يَوْمَئِذٍ - أَذْكُرُ لَهُ ذَلِكَ فَكَتَبَ إِلَىَّ عُمَرُ أَنْ أَجِزْ عَفْوَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ زُرَيْقٌ وَكَتَبْتُ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ أَيْضًا أَرَأَيْتَ رَجُلاً افْتُرِيَ عَلَيْهِ أَوْ عَلَى أَبَوَيْهِ وَقَدْ هَلَكَا أَوْ أَحَدُهُمَا ‏.‏ قَالَ فَكَتَبَ إِلَىَّ عُمَرُ إِنْ عَفَا فَأَجِزْ عَفْوَهُ فِي نَفْسِهِ وَإِنِ افْتُرِيَ عَلَى أَبَوَيْهِ وَقَدْ هَلَكَا أَوْ أَحَدُهُمَا فَخُذْ لَهُ بِكِتَابِ اللَّهِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يُرِيدَ سِتْرًا ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ وَذَلِكَ أَنْ يَكُونَ الرَّجُلُ الْمُفْتَرَى عَلَيْهِ يَخَافُ إِنْ كُشِفَ ذَلِكَ مِنْهُ أَنْ تَقُومَ عَلَيْهِ بَيِّنَةٌ فَإِذَا كَانَ عَلَى مَا وَصَفْتُ فَعَفَا جَازَ عَفْوُهُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al- Aziz (successor). The source narrates two correspondence instances involving Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz. Preserve them as separate report groups and retain the source&#x27;s differently spaced name strings. Malik&#x27;s subsequent explanatory comment is excluded from both quotations. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). The source narrates two correspondence instances involving Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz. Preserve them as separate report groups and retain the source&#x27;s differently spaced name strings. Malik&#x27;s subsequent explanatory comment is excluded from both quotations.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Zurayq ibn Hakim al-Ayli that a man called Misbah asked his son for help and he thought him unnecessarily slow. When the son came, his father said to him, &quot;O fornicator.&quot; Zurayq said, &quot;So the son asked me to help him against the father. When I wanted to flog him, his son said, &#x27;By Allah, if you flog him, I will acknowledge that I have committed fornication.&#x27; When he said that, the situation was confused for me, so I wrote about it to Umar ibn Abd al- Aziz who was the governor at that time, and I mentioned it to him. Umar wrote me to permit his pardon.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Zurayq said, &quot;I wrote to Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz also, &#x27;What do you think about a man who is slandered or his parents are slandered and both or only one of them are dead?&#x27; He said, Umar wrote to me, &#x27;If he forgives, his pardon is permitted for himself. If his parents are slandered and one or both of them are dead, take the judgement of the Book of Allah for it unless he wants to veil it.&#x27; &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1517]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1517" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said, &quot;I heard Malik say, &#x27;That is because the slandered man might fear that if that is unveiled about him, a clear proof might be established. If it is according to what we have described, his pardon is permitted</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ारीक़ बिन हाकिम से रिवायत है के एक शख्स ने जिसका नाम मिस्बाह था अपने बेटे को किसी काम के वास्ते बुलाया की. जब आया तोह मिस्बाह ने कहा के ऐ जानि! ज़ारीक़ ने कहा उस लड़के ने मेरे पास फर्याद की. मैं ने जब उसके बाप को हद मारनी चाही तोह वह लड़का बोला: अगर तुम मेरे बाप को कोड़ों से मरोगे तोह मैं जीने का इकरार कर लूंगा. मैं यह सुन कर हैरान हुआ और इस मुक़दमे का फ़ैसला करना मुझ पर दुश्वार हुआ में हाकिम थे मदीना के (सुलईमान बिन अब्दुल मलिक की तरफ से). उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने जवाब लिखा के लड़के के अफ़व को जाइज़ रख (यानि बेटे ने अगर बाप को हाद माफ़ कार्डी है तोह अफ़व सही है). ज़ारीक़ ने कहा मैं ने उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ को यह भी लिखा था के अगर कोई शख़्स दुसरे पर तोहमत-इ-ज़िना की लगाये या उसके माँ-बाप को हों या दोनों में से एक मर गया हो तोहमत-इ-ज़िना की लगाये अगर वह माफ़ कर दे तोह अफ़व दुरुस्त है उसका अफ़व कर देना दुरुस्त नहीं जबकि वालिदैन मर गए हों या उन दो में से एक मर गया हो किताब-इ-अल्लाह के. मगर जब बेटा अपने वालिदैन का हाल छुपाने के वास्ते अफ़व कर दे तोह अफ़व दुरुस्त है. कहा मालिक ने: यानि उसको खौफ हो अगर मैं तोहमत लगाने वाले को माफ न करूँगा तोह वालिदैन का ज़िना गवाहों से साबित हो जायेगा वजह से अफ़व करदे तोह अफ़व दुरुस्त है.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زریق بن حکیم سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے جس کا نام مصباح تھا اپنے بیٹے کو کسی کام کے واسطے بلایا اس نے دیر کی جب آیا تو مصباح نے کہا کہ اے زانی، کہا زریق نے اس لڑکے نے میرے پاس فریاد کی میں نے جب اس کے باپ کو حد مارنی چاہی تو وہ لڑکا بولا اگر تم میرے باپ کو کوڑوں سے مارو گے تو میں زنا کا اقرار کر لوں گا میں یہ سن کر حیران ہوا اور اس مقدمے کا فیصلہ کرنا مجھ پر دشوار ہوا تو میں نے عمر بن عبدالعزیز کو لکھا وہ اس زمانے میں حاکم تھے مدینہ کے (سلمان بن عبدالملک کی طرف سے) عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے جواب لکھا کہ لڑکے کے عفو کو جائز رکھ(یعنی بیٹے نے اگر باپ کو حد معاف کردی ہے تو عفو صحیح ہے) زریق نے کہا میں نے عمربن عبدالعزیز کو یہ بھی لکھا تھا کہ اگر کوئی شخص دوسرے پر تہمت زنا کی لگائے یا اس کے ماں باپ کو اور ماں باپ اس کے مر گئے ہوں یا دونوں میں سے ایک مر گیا ہو تو پھر کیا کرے، عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے جواب میں لکھا کہ جس شخص کو تہمت زنا کی لگائے اگر وہ معاف کر دے تو عفو درست ہے لیکن اگر اس کے والدین کو تہمت زنا کی لگائے تو اس کا عفو کر دینا درست نہیں جب کہ والدین مر گئے ہوں یا ان دو میں سے ایک مر گیا ہو بلکہ حد لگا اس کو موافق کتاب اللہ کے مگر جب بیٹا اپنے والدین کا حال چھپانے کے واسطے عفو کر دے تو عفو درست ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے یعنی اس کو خوف ہو اگر میں تہمت لگانے والے کو معاف نہ کروں گا تو والدین کا زنا گواہوں سے ثابت ہوجائے گا اس وجہ سے عفو کردے تو عفو درست ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zariq bin Hakim se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne jiska naam Misbah tha apne bete ko kisi kaam ke waste bulaya, usne der ki. Jab aaya toh Misbah ne kaha ke ae zani! Zariq ne kaha us ladke ne mere paas faryad ki. Main ne jab uske baap ko had marni chahi toh woh ladka bola: Agar tum mere baap ko kodon se maroge toh main zina ka iqrar kar lunga. Main yeh sun kar hairan hua aur is muqadme ka faisla karna mujh par dushwar hua, toh main ne Umar bin Abdul Aziz ko likha, woh us zamane mein hakim the Madina ke (Sulaiman bin Abdul Malik ki taraf se). Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne jawab likha ke ladke ke afw ko jaiz rakh (yani bete ne agar baap ko had maaf kardi hai toh afw sahi hai). Zariq ne kaha main ne Umar bin Abdul Aziz ko yeh bhi likha tha ke agar koi shakhs dusre par tohmat-e-zina ki lagaye ya uske maa-baap ko, aur maa-baap uske mar gaye hon ya dono mein se ek mar gaya ho, toh phir kya kare? Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne jawab mein likha ke jis shakhs ko tohmat-e-zina ki lagaye agar woh maaf kar de toh afw durust hai, lekin agar uske walidain ko tohmat-e-zina ki lagaye toh uska afw kar dena durust nahi jabke walidain mar gaye hon ya un do mein se ek mar gaya ho, balki had laga usko muafiq kitab-e-Allah ke. Magar jab beta apne walidain ka haal chupane ke waste afw kar de toh afw durust hai. Kaha Malik ne: Yani usko khauf ho agar main tohmat lagane wale ko maaf na karunga toh walidain ka zina gawahon se sabit ho jayega, is wajah se afw karde toh afw durust hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1517" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1518]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1518" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ فِي رَجُلٍ قَذَفَ قَوْمًا جَمَاعَةً أَنَّهُ لَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ إِلاَّ حَدٌّ وَاحِدٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنْ تَفَرَّقُوا فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ إِلاَّ حَدٌّ وَاحِدٌ ‏.‏ حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ أَبِي الرِّجَالِ، مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ حَارِثَةَ بْنِ النُّعْمَانِ الأَنْصَارِيِّ ثُمَّ مِنْ بَنِي النَّجَّارِ عَنْ أُمِّهِ، عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّ رَجُلَيْنِ، اسْتَبَّا فِي زَمَانِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَ أَحَدُهُمَا لِلآخَرِ وَاللَّهِ مَا أَبِي بِزَانٍ وَلاَ أُمِّي بِزَانِيَةٍ ‏.‏ فَاسْتَشَارَ فِي ذَلِكَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَ قَائِلٌ مَدَحَ أَبَاهُ وَأُمَّهُ وَقَالَ آخَرُونَ قَدْ كَانَ لأَبِيهِ وَأُمِّهِ مَدْحٌ غَيْرُ هَذَا نَرَى أَنْ تَجْلِدَهُ الْحَدَّ ‏.‏ فَجَلَدَهُ عُمَرُ الْحَدَّ ثَمَانِينَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ حَدَّ عِنْدَنَا إِلاَّ فِي نَفْىٍ أَوْ قَذْفٍ أَوْ تَعْرِيضٍ يُرَى أَنَّ قَائِلَهُ إِنَّمَا أَرَادَ بِذَلِكَ نَفْيًا أَوْ قَذْفًا فَعَلَى مَنْ قَالَ ذَلِكَ الْحَدُّ تَامًّا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ إِذَا نَفَى رَجُلٌ رَجُلاً مِنْ أَبِيهِ فَإِنَّ عَلَيْهِ الْحَدَّ وَإِنْ كَانَتْ أُمُّ الَّذِي نُفِيَ مَمْلُوكَةً فَإِنَّ عَلَيْهِ الْحَدَّ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). Two transmitted reports are separated from Malik&#x27;s intervening and concluding commentary. The first attributes a statement to Hisham&#x27;s father without supplying his name in this field; that name remains unresolved. The second reports Umar&#x27;s consultation and action. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Two transmitted reports are separated from Malik&#x27;s intervening and concluding commentary. The first attributes a statement to Hisham&#x27;s father without supplying his name in this field; that name remains unresolved. The second reports Umar&#x27;s consultation and action.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Hisham ibn Urwa that his father said that there was only one hadd against a man who slandered a group of people.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Abu&#x27;r-Rijal Muhammad ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Haritha ibn an-Numan al- Ansari, then from the Banu&#x27;n-Najar from his mother Amra bint Abd ar- Rahman that two men cursed each other in the time of Umar ibn al- Khattab. One of them said to the other, &quot; By Allah, my father is not an adulterer and my mother is not an adulteress.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab asked advice about that. One person said, &quot;He has praised his father and mother.&quot; Another said, &quot;His father and mother have praise other than this. We think that he is to be flogged with the hadd.&quot; So Umar flogged him with the hadd of eighty lashes.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1518]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1518" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1518 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;If they are on separate occasions there is still only one hadd against him.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1518" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;There is no hadd in our view except for slander, denial or insinuation, in which one sees that the speaker intends by that denial or slander. Then the hadd is completely imposed on the one who said it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community when a man denies that another man is from his father, is that he deserves the hadd. If the mother who is denied is a slave, then he deserves the hadd as well</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1518" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1503

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1520)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1520]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1520</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1520 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ لِرَجُلٍ خَرَجَ بِجَارِيَةٍ لاِمْرَأَتِهِ مَعَهُ فِي سَفَرٍ فَأَصَابَهَا فَغَارَتِ امْرَأَتُهُ ‏.‏ فَذَكَرَتْ ذَلِكَ لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَسَأَلَهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ وَهَبَتْهَا لِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ لَتَأْتِينِي بِالْبَيِّنَةِ أَوْ لأَرْمِيَنَّكَ بِالْحِجَارَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَاعْتَرَفَتِ امْرَأَتُهُ أَنَّهَا وَهَبَتْهَا لَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1520" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). Select Rabia&#x27;s transmitted account of the case brought to Umar and the wife&#x27;s subsequent acknowledgement. Malik&#x27;s preceding legal discussion remains outside the report.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman that Umar ibn al-Khattab spoke about a man who went out with his wife&#x27;s slave- girl on a journey and had intercourse with her and then the wife became jealous and mentioned that to Umar ibn al-Khattab. Umar questioned him about it. He said, &quot;She gave her to me.&quot; Umar said, &quot;Bring me a clear proof or I will stone you.&quot; Rabia added, &quot;The wife confessed that she had given her to him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1520]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1520" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1520</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1520 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;The best of what is heard about a slave-girl whom a man has intercourse with while he has a partner in her is that the hadd is not inflicted on him and the child is connected to him. When the slave-girl becomes pregnant, her value is estimated and he gives his partners their shares of the price and the slave-girl is his. That is what is done among us.&quot; Malik said about a man who made his slave-girl halal to a man that if the one for whom she was made halal had intercourse with her, her value was estimated on the day he had intercourse with her and he owed that to her owner whether or not she conceived. The hadd was averted from him by that. If she conceived the child was connected to him. Malik said about a man who had intercourse with his son&#x27;s or daughter&#x27;s slave-girl, &quot;The hadd is averted from him and he owes the estimated value of the slave-girl whether or not she conceives.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1520" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राबिया बिन अबू अब्दुल रहमान (रज़िअल्लाहु अन्हु) रिवायत है के एक शख़्स अपनी बीवी की लोंदी को सफ़र में साथ ले कर निकला, वहां उस से सोहबत की। औरत ने रश्क के मारे हज़रत उमर (रज़िअल्लाहु अन्हु) से कह दिया। हज़रत उमर (रज़िअल्लाहु अन्हु) ने मर्द से पूछा, वह बोला के औरत ने इस लोंदी को मुझे हिबा (गिफ़्ट) करदिया था। हज़रत उमर (रज़िअल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा या तो तू गवाह ला हिबा के नहीं तो तुझे रज़म करूंगा। उस वक़्त औरत ने कह दिया के हां मैं ने हिबा कर दिया था।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1520" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن ابو عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص اپنی بیوی کی لونڈی کو سفر میں ساتھ لے کر نکلا وہاں اس سے صحبت کی عورت نے رشک کے مارے حضرت عمر سے کہہ دیا حضرت عمر نے مرد سے پوچھا وہ بولا کہ عورت نے اس لونڈی کو مجھے ہبہ کردیا تھا حضرت عمر نے کہا یا تو تو گواہ لا ہبہ کے نہیں تو تجھے رجم کروں گا۔ اس وقت عورت نے کہہ دیا کہ ہاں میں نے ہبہ کر دیا تھا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1520" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abu Abdul Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) riwayat hai ke ek shakhs apni biwi ki londi ko safar mein saath le kar nikla, wahan us se sohbat ki. Aurat ne rashk ke maare Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se keh diya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne mard se pucha, woh bola ke aurat ne is londi ko mujhe hiba (gift) kardiya tha. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha ya toh tu gawah la hiba ke nahi toh tujhe rajam karunga. Us waqt aurat ne keh diya ke haan main ne hiba kar diya tha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1520" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1504

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1521)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَطَعَ فِي مِجَنٍّ ثَمَنُهُ ثَلاَثَةُ دَرَاهِمَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1521]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1521" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, cut off the hand of a man who stole a shield whose price was three dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1521]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1521" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1505

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1522)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ أَبِي حُسَيْنٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ قَطْعَ فِي ثَمَرٍ مُعَلَّقٍ وَلاَ فِي حَرِيسَةِ جَبَلٍ ‏&quot;‏ فَإِذَا آوَاهُ الْمُرَاحُ أَوِ الْجَرِينُ فَالْقَطْعُ فِيمَا يَبْلُغُ ثَمَنَ الْمِجَنِّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1522</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1522 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abd ar-Rahman abu Husayn al-Makki that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The hand is not cut off for fruit hanging on the tree and for sheep kept in the mountains. So when they are taken from the fold or the place where the fruit is dried, a hand is cut off for whatever reaches the price of a shield</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्दुल रहमान मक्की से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया कि जो मेवा दरख़्त पर लटकता हो या जो बकरी पहाड़ पर फिरती हो उसके उठा लेने में हाथ न काटा जायेगा मगर जब वह बकरी अपने घर में आ जाये या मेवा काट कर खाने को कहीं रखा जाये फिर उसको कोई चुराये तोह हाथ काटा जायेगा बशर्त-इ-के क़ीमत उसकी ढाल के बराबर हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عبدالرحمن مکی سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ جو میوہ درخت پر لٹکتا ہو یا جو بکری پہاڑ پر پھرتی ہو اس کے اٹھا لینے میں ہاتھ نہ کاٹا جائے گا مگر جب وہ بکری اپنے گھر میں آجائے یا میوہ کاٹ کر کھانے کو کہیں رکھا جائے پھر اس کو کوئی چرائے تو ہاتھ کاٹا جائے گا بشرطیکہ قیمت اس کی ڈھال کے برابر ہو ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abdul Rahman Makki se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke jo mewa darakht par latakta ho ya jo bakri pahaad par phirti ho uske utha lene mein haath na kaata jayega magar jab woh bakri apne ghar mein aa jaye ya mewa kaat kar khane ko kahin rakha jaye phir usko koi churaye toh haath kaata jayega bashart-e-ke qeemat uski dhaal ke barabar ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1522]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1522" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1506

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1523)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1523]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1523" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1523</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1523 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ سَارِقًا، سَرَقَ فِي زَمَانِ عُثْمَانَ أُتْرُجَّةً فَأَمَرَ بِهَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ عَفَّانَ أَنْ تُقَوَّمَ فَقُوِّمَتْ بِثَلاَثَةِ دَرَاهِمَ مِنْ صَرْفِ اثْنَىْ عَشَرَ دِرْهَمًا بِدِينَارٍ فَقَطَعَ عُثْمَانُ يَدَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1523" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Uthman ibn Affan (companion). The source reports a valuation and action attributed to Uthman. Retain the original amounts and source narrative as a Companion report.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr from his father from Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that a thief stole a citron in the time of Uthman Uthman ibn Affan ordered its value to be estimated and it was estimated at three dirhams at the rate of exchange of twelve dirhams for the dinar, so Uthman cut off his hand</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1523]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1523" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत अमरा बिंत अब्दुल रहमान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के एक शख़्स ने हज़रत उस्मान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के ज़माने में तुरंज (सिट्रस) चुराया। हज़रत उस्मान ने उसकी क़ीमत लगवायी, वो तीन दिरहम का निकला, 12 दिरहम फ़ी दीनार के हिसाब से, तो हज़रत उस्मान ने उसका हाथ काटा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1523" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عمرہ بنت عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے حضرت عثمان رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے زمانے میں ترنج چرایا حضرت عثمان نے اس کی قیمت لگوائی وہ تین درہم کا نکلا بارہ درہم فی دینار کے حساب سے تو حضرت عثمان نے اس کا ہاتھ کاٹا۔</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Amra bint Abdul Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs ne Hazrat Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke zamane mein turanj (citrus) churaya. Hazrat Usman ne uski qeemat lagwayi, woh teen dirham ka nikla, 12 dirham fi dinar ke hisab se, toh Hazrat Usman ne uska haath kaata.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ مَا طَالَ عَلَىَّ وَمَا نَسِيتُ ‏ &quot;‏ الْقَطْعُ فِي رُبُعِ دِينَارٍ فَصَاعِدًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1524" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The statement is expressly Aisha&#x27;s recollection and wording. Her description as the Prophet&#x27;s wife does not change the speaker attribution to the Prophet.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;It has not been a long time for me and I have not forgotten. A thief&#x27;s hand is cut off for a quarter of a dinar and upwards</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1524]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1524" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1525]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1525</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1525 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ حَزْمٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ خَرَجَتْ عَائِشَةُ زَوْجُ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَى مَكَّةَ وَمَعَهَا مَوْلاَتَانِ لَهَا وَمَعَهَا غُلاَمٌ لِبَنِي عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَبَعَثَتْ مَعَ الْمَوْلاَتَيْنِ بِبُرْدٍ مُرَجَّلٍ قَدْ خِيطَ عَلَيْهِ خِرْقَةٌ خَضْرَاءُ قَالَتْ فَأَخَذَ الْغُلاَمُ الْبُرْدَ فَفَتَقَ عَنْهُ فَاسْتَخْرَجَهُ وَجَعَلَ مَكَانَهُ لِبْدًا أَوْ فَرْوَةً وَخَاطَ عَلَيْهِ فَلَمَّا قَدِمَتِ الْمَوْلاَتَانِ الْمَدِينَةَ دَفَعَتَا ذَلِكَ إِلَى أَهْلِهِ فَلَمَّا فَتَقُوا عَنْهُ وَجَدُوا فِيهِ اللِّبْدَ وَلَمْ يَجِدُوا الْبُرْدَ فَكَلَّمُوا الْمَرْأَتَيْنِ فَكَلَّمَتَا عَائِشَةَ زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَوْ كَتَبَتَا إِلَيْهَا وَاتَّهَمَتَا الْعَبْدَ فَسُئِلَ الْعَبْدُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَاعْتَرَفَ فَأَمَرَتْ بِهِ عَائِشَةُ زَوْجُ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقُطِعَتْ يَدُهُ وَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ الْقَطْعُ فِي رُبُعِ دِينَارٍ فَصَاعِدًا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. The selected passage is a locally explicit Prophetic report. Surrounding companion, jurist, compiler and explanatory source material remains under Comments. No source wording is altered and no authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, cut off the hand of a thief for a shield whose value was three dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1525]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1525</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1525 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Hazim that Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman said, &quot;A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, went out to Makka and she had two girl mawlas of hers and a slave belonging to the sons of Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr as-Siddiq . She sent a figured cloak with the two mawlas which was sewn up in a piece of green cloth.&quot; Amra continued, &quot;The slave took it and unstitched it and took out the cloak. In its place, he put some felt or skin and sewed it up again. When the mawla girls came to Madina, they gave it to his people. When they opened it, they found felt in it and did not find the cloak. They spoke to the two women and they spoke to A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, or they wrote to her and suspected the slave. The slave was asked about it and confessed. A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave the order and his hand was cut off. A&#x27;isha said, &#x27;A thief&#x27;s hand is cut off for a quarter of a dinar and upwards.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;The limit I prefer above which cutting off the hand is obliged is three dirhams, whether the exchange is high or low. That is because </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">, and Uthman ibn Affan cut off the hand of a thief for a citron which was estimated at three dirhams. This is what I prefer of what I have heard on the matter</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1525]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1525</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1525 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अम्र बिन्त अब्दुल रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के हज़रात उम्-उल-मोमिनीन ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) मक्काः गयीं. उनके साथ दो लोंड़ियाँ थी (उनकी आज़ाद की हुई) और एक ग़ुलाम था अब्दुल्लाह बिन अबू बक्र की औलाद का. हज़रात ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) ने मक्काः से उन दो लौंडियों के हाथ एक चादर भेजी जिसमें तस्वीरें बनी हुई थीं मर्दों की. एक सब्ज़ कपडे में लपेट कर सी दिया था. उस ग़ुलाम ने क्या किया उसकी जगह एक ठेला पोस्तीन रख दी और फिर सी दिया. जब वह लोंड़ियाँ मदीना को आयीं और वह अमानत जिन को हज़रात ऐशा ने कहा था के सुपुर्द की हज़रात ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) को बयां किया या उनको लिख भेजा और अपना गुमां घुलम पर ज़ाहिर किया. जब घुलम से पुछा गया तोह उसने इक़रार किया. हज़रात ऐशा ने उसके हाथ काटने का हुक्म दिया फ़रमाया रब-इ-डिनर या ज़यादा में हाथ काटा जाता है.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عمرہ بنت عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ حضرت ام المومنین عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا مکہ کو گئیں ان کے ساتھ دو لونڈیاں تھیں ان کی آزاد کی ہوئیں اور ایک غلام تھا عبداللہ بن ابی بکر کی اولاد کا حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا نے مکہ سے ان دو لونڈیوں کے ہاتھ ایک چادر بھیجی جس میں تصویریں بنی ہوئی تھیں مردوں کی۔ ایک سبز کپڑے میں لپیٹ کر سی دیا تھا اس غلام نے کیا کیا کپڑے کی سیون ادھیڑ کر اس میں سے چادر نکال لی اور اس کی جگہ ایک تھیلا پوستین رکھ دی اور پھر سی دیا جب وہ لونڈیاں مدینہ کو آئیں اور وہ امانت جن کو حضرت عائشہ نے کہا تھا کہ سپرد کی انہوں نے اڈھیر کر دیکھا تو نمدہ ہے چادر نہیں ہے لونڈیوں سے پوچھا لونڈیوں نے حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا بیان کیا یا ان کولکھ بھیجا اور اپنا گمان غلام پر ظاہر کیا جب غلام سے پوچھا گیا تو اس نے اقرار کیا حضرت عائشہ نے اس کے ہاتھ کاٹنے کا حکم کیا اس کا ہاتھ کاٹا گیا حضرت عائشہ نے فرمایا ربع دینار یا زیادہ میں ہاتھ کاٹا جاتا ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1525]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1525</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1525 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Amra bint Abdul Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umm-ul-Momineen Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) Makkah gayin. Unke saath do londiyan thi (unki azad ki hui) aur ek ghulam tha Abdullah bin Abu Bakr ki aulad ka. Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) ne Makkah se un do londiyon ke haath ek chadar bheji jismein tasweerein bani hui thin mardon ki. Ek sabz kapde mein lapet kar si diya tha. Us ghulam ne kya kiya, kapde ki seewan adhed kar usmein se chadar nikal li aur uski jagah ek thela posteen rakh di aur phir si diya. Jab woh londiyan Madina ko aayin aur woh amanat jin ko Hazrat Aisha ne kaha tha ke supurd ki, unhone adhed kar dekha toh namda hai, chadar nahi hai. Londiyon se poocha, londiyon ne Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) ko bayan kiya ya unko likh bheja aur apna guman ghulam par zahir kiya. Jab ghulam se poocha gaya toh usne iqrar kiya. Hazrat Aisha ne uske haath kaatne ka hukm diya, uska haath kaata gaya. Hazrat Aisha ne farmaya rub-e-dinar ya zyada mein haath kaata jata hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1525" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1509

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدًا، لِعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ سَرَقَ وَهُوَ آبِقٌ فَأَرْسَلَ بِهِ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ إِلَى سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْعَاصِ - وَهُوَ أَمِيرُ الْمَدِينَةِ - لِيَقْطَعَ يَدَهُ فَأَبَى سَعِيدٌ أَنْ يَقْطَعَ يَدَهُ وَقَالَ لاَ تُقْطَعُ يَدُ الآبِقِ السَّارِقِ إِذَا سَرَقَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ فِي أَىِّ كِتَابِ اللَّهِ وَجَدْتَ هَذَا ثُمَّ أَمَرَ بِهِ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ فَقُطِعَتْ يَدُهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1526" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source reports the disagreement between Abdullah ibn Umar and Said ibn al-As and the subsequent action. Preserve the dialogue with its own source attribution.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that a slave of Abdullah ibn Umar stole while he was a runaway. Abdullah ibn Umar sent him to Said ibn al-As, who was the amir of Madina, to cut off his hand. Said refused to cut off his hand. He said, &quot;The hand of a runaway slave is not cut off when he steals.&quot; Abdullah ibn Umar said to him, &quot;In what Book of Allah did you find this?&quot; Then Abdullah ibn Umar gave the order, and his hand was cut off</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1526]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1526" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नफ़ी से रिवायत है के एक घुलम अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र का भागा हुआ था उसने चोरी की. अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने उस ग़ुलाम को सईद बिन अल-ास के पास भेजा जो हाकिम थे मदीना के जाये तोह उसका हाथ नहीं काटा जाता. अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने कहा कि तू ने यह हुक्म किस किताब-इ-अल्लाह में पाया? फिर अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर ने हुक्म किया और उसका हाथ काटा गया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1526" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke ek ghulam Abdullah bin Umar ka bhaga hua tha usne chori ki. Abdullah bin Umar ne us ghulam ko Saeed bin Al-Aas ke paas bheja jo hakim the Madina ke, haath kaatne ko. Saeed bin Aas ne na maana aur kaha jab koi bhaag jaye toh uska haath nahi kaata jata. Abdullah bin Umar ne kaha ke tu ne yeh hukm kis kitab-e-Allah mein paya? Phir Abdullah bin Umar ne hukm kiya aur uska haath kaata gaya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1526" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1510

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1527)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1527]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1527" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1527</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1527 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زُرَيْقِ بْنِ حَكِيمٍ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ، أَخَذَ عَبْدًا آبِقًا قَدْ سَرَقَ قَالَ فَأَشْكَلَ عَلَىَّ أَمْرُهُ - قَالَ - فَكَتَبْتُ فِيهِ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ أَسْأَلُهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ وَهُوَ الْوَالِي يَوْمَئِذٍ - قَالَ - فَأَخْبَرْتُهُ أَنَّنِي كُنْتُ أَسْمَعُ أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ الآبِقَ إِذَا سَرَقَ وَهُوَ آبِقٌ لَمْ تُقْطَعْ يَدُهُ - قَالَ - فَكَتَبَ إِلَىَّ عُمَرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ نَقِيضَ كِتَابِي يَقُولُ كَتَبْتَ إِلَىَّ أَنَّكَ كُنْتَ تَسْمَعُ أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ الآبِقَ إِذَا سَرَقَ لَمْ تُقْطَعْ يَدُهُ وَأَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى يَقُولُ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَالسَّارِقُ وَالسَّارِقَةُ فَاقْطَعُوا أَيْدِيَهُمَا جَزَاءً بِمَا كَسَبَا نَكَالاً مِنَ اللَّهِ وَاللَّهُ عَزِيزٌ حَكِيمٌ‏}‏ ‏.‏ فَإِنْ بَلَغَتْ سَرِقَتُهُ رُبُعَ دِينَارٍ فَصَاعِدًا فَاقْطَعْ يَدَهُ ‏.‏ وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ الْقَاسِمَ بْنَ مُحَمَّدٍ، وَسَالِمَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، وَعُرْوَةَ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، كَانُوا يَقُولُونَ إِذَا سَرَقَ الْعَبْدُ الآبِقُ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ قُطِعَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ الأَمْرُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ الآبِقَ إِذَا سَرَقَ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ قُطِعَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1527" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). Keep two distinct transmissions: the correspondence with Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz and a joint statement attributed to al-Qasim, Salim and Urwa. Preserve the Quran quotation inside the letter, but retain its printed parenthetical locator separately without correcting or promoting that supplied locator. Malik&#x27;s final commentary stays outside both reports. Report type: successor report. Attribution: al- Qasim ibn Muhammad (successor); Salim ibn Abdullah (successor); Urwa ibn az-Zubayr (successor). Keep two distinct transmissions: the correspondence with Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz and a joint statement attributed to al-Qasim, Salim and Urwa. Preserve the Quran quotation inside the letter, but retain its printed parenthetical locator separately without correcting or promoting that supplied locator. Malik&#x27;s final commentary stays outside both reports. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Zurayq ibn Hakim informed him that he had a runaway slave who had stolen. He said, &quot;The situation was obscure for me, so I wrote to Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz to ask him about it. He was the governor at that time. I informed him that I had heard that if a runaway slave stole while he was a fugitive, his hand was not cut off. &#x27;Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz wrote to contradict my letter, &#x27;You wrote to me that you have heard that when the runaway slave steals, his hand is not cut off. Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;The thief, male and female, cut off the hands of both, as a recompense for what they have earned, and an exemplary punishment from Allah. Allah is Mighty, Wise.&#x27;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> When his theft reaches a quarter of a dinar, and upwards, his hand is cut off.&#x27; &quot; </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that al- Qasim ibn Muhammad and Salim ibn Abdullah and Urwa ibn az-Zubayr said, &quot;When a runaway slave steals something for which cutting off the hand is obliged, his hand is cut off.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1527]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1527" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ुरैक बिन हाकिम ने एक भागे हुए ग़ुलाम को गिरफ़्तार किया जिसने चोरी की थी। फिर उनको यह मसला मुश्किल मालूम हुआ, उन्होंने उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ को लिखा (वो उस ज़माने में अमीर-उल-मोमिनीन थे) और यह भी लिखा के मैं सुनता था जो ग़ुलाम भाग जाए फिर वो चोरी करे तो उसका हाथ न काटा जाए। उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने जवाब में लिखा और मेरी तहरीर का हवाला दिया और कहा के तू ने लिखा है के तू सुनता था जो ग़ुलाम भागा हुआ हो वो चोरी करे तो उसका हाथ न काटा जाएगा, हालाँकि अल्लाह ने फ़रमाया है के जो मर्द चोरी करे या औरत चोरी करे तो उनके हाथ काटो, यह बदला है उनके काम का और अज़ाब है अल्लाह की तरफ़ से, अल्लाह ग़ालिब है हिकमत वाला। पस अगर उस ग़ुलाम ने रूब-ए-दीनार के मुवाफ़िक़ या उस से ज़्यादा चोरी की हो, उसका हाथ काट डाल। क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद और सालिम बिन अब्दुल्लाह और उरवा बिन ज़ुबैर कहते थे भागा हुआ ग़ुलाम जब इस क़दर चुराये जिस में हाथ काटना वाजिब होता है तो उसका हाथ काटा जाएगा। कहा मालिक ने, हमारे नज़दीक इस में कुछ इख़्तिलाफ़ नहीं है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1527" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زر یق بن حکیم نے ایک بھاگے ہوئے غلام کو گر فتار کیا جس نے چوری کی تھی پھر ان کو یہ مسئلہ مشکل معلوم ہوا انہوں نے عمر بن عبدالعزیز کو لکھا وہ اس زمانے میں امیرالمو منین تھے اور یہ بھی لکھا کہ میں سنتا تھا جو غلام بھاگ جائے پھر وہ چوری کرے تو اس کا ہاتھ نہ کاٹا جائے عمر بن عبد العز یز نے جواب میں لکھا اور میری تحریر کا حوالہ دیا اور کہا کہ تو نے لکھا ہے کہ تو سنتا تھا جو غلام بھاگا ہوا ہو وہ چوری کرے تو اس کا ہاتھ نہ کاٹا جائے گا حالانکہ اللہ نے فرمایا ہے کہ جو مرد چوری کرے یا عورت چوری کرے تو ان کے ہاتھ کاٹو یہ بدلہ ہے ان کے کام کا اور عذاب ہے اللہ کی طرف سے اللہ غالب ہے حکمت والا پس اگر اس غلام نے ربع دینار کے موافق یا اس سے زیادہ چوری کی ہو اس کا ہاتھ کاٹ ڈال۔ قاسم بن محمد اور سالم بن عبداللہ اور عروہ بن الزبیر کہتے تھے بھاگا ہوا غلام جب اس قدر چرائے جس میں ہاتھ کاٹنا واجب ہوتا ہے تو اس کا ہاتھ کاٹا جائے گا۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک اس میں کچھ اختلاف نہیں ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1527" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zuraiq bin Hakim ne ek bhage hue ghulam ko giraftar kiya jisne chori ki thi. Phir unko yeh masla mushkil maloom hua, unhone Umar bin Abdul Aziz ko likha (woh us zamane mein Amir-ul-Momineen the) aur yeh bhi likha ke main sunta tha jo ghulam bhaag jaye phir woh chori kare toh uska haath na kaata jaye. Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne jawab mein likha aur meri tehrir ka hawala diya aur kaha ke tu ne likha hai ke tu sunta tha jo ghulam bhaga hua ho woh chori kare toh uska haath na kaata jayega, halankeh Allah ne farmaya hai ke jo mard chori kare ya aurat chori kare toh unke haath kaato, yeh badla hai unke kaam ka aur azab hai Allah ki taraf se, Allah ghalib hai hikmat wala. Pas agar us ghulam ne rub-e-dinar ke muwafiq ya us se zyada chori ki ho, uska haath kaat daal. Qasim bin Muhammad aur Salim bin Abdullah aur Urwah bin Zubair kehte the bhaga hua ghulam jab is qadar churaye jis mein haath kaatna wajib hota hai toh uska haath kaata jayega. Kaha Malik ne, hamare nazdeek is mein kuch ikhtilaf nahi hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1527" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1511

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1529)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ صَفْوَانَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ صَفْوَانَ، أَنَّ صَفْوَانَ بْنَ أُمَيَّةَ، قِيلَ لَهُ إِنَّهُ مَنْ لَمْ يُهَاجِرْ هَلَكَ ‏.‏ فَقَدِمَ صَفْوَانُ بْنُ أُمَيَّةَ الْمَدِينَةَ فَنَامَ فِي الْمَسْجِدِ وَتَوَسَّدَ رِدَاءَهُ فَجَاءَ سَارِقٌ فَأَخَذَ رِدَاءَهُ فَأَخَذَ صَفْوَانُ السَّارِقَ فَجَاءَ بِهِ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَسَرَقْتَ رِدَاءَ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ تُقْطَعَ يَدُهُ فَقَالَ لَهُ صَفْوَانُ إِنِّي لَمْ أُرِدْ هَذَا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ هُوَ عَلَيْهِ صَدَقَةٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ فَهَلاَّ قَبْلَ أَنْ تَأْتِيَنِي بِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Safwan ibn Abdullah ibn Safwan that it was said to Safwan ibn Umayya, &quot;Whoever does not do hijra is ruined.&quot; So Safwan ibn Umayya went to Madina and slept in the mosque with his cloak as a pillow. A thief came and took his cloak and Safwan grabbed hold of the thief and brought him to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;Did you steal this cloak?&quot; He said, &quot;Yes.&quot; So the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ordered that his hand be cut off. Safwan said to him, &quot;I did not intend this. It is his as sadaqa.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Why didn&#x27;t you do it before bringing him to me?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सफ़वान बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन सफ़वान से रिवायत है के सफ़वान बिन उमय्या से किसी ने कहा के जिसने हिजरत नहीं की तो वो तबाह हुआ, तो सफ़वान मदीना में आये और मस्जिद-ए-नबवी में अपनी चादर सर के तले रख कर सो रहे। चोर आया और चादर उनकी ले गया। सफ़वान ने उठ कर चोर को गिरफ़्तार किया और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास ले आये। आपने चोर से पूछा के क्या तू ने सफ़वान की चादर चुरायी? वो बोला हाँ। आपने उसका हाथ काटने का हुक्म किया। सफ़वान ने कहा मेरी नियत यह न थी या रसूल अल्लाह, वो चादर तो इस पर सदक़ा है। आपने फ़रमाया तुझको यह अमर मेरे पास लाने से पहले करना था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">صفوان بن عبداللہ بن صفوان سے روایت ہے کہ صفوان بن امیہ سے کسی نے کہا کہ جس نے ہجرت نہیں کی تو وہ تباہ ہوا تو صفوان مدینہ میں آئے اور مسجد نبوی میں اپنی چادر سر کے تلے رکھ کر سو رہے چور آیا اور چادر ان کی لے گیا صفوان نے اٹھ کر چور کو گرفتار کیا اور رسول اللہ کے پاس لے آئے آپ نے چور سے پوچھا کہ کیا تو نے صفوان کی چادر چرائی وہ بولا ہاں آپ نے اس کا ہاتھ کاٹنے کا حکم کیا صفوان نے کہا میری نیت یہ نہ تھی یا رسول اللہ وہ چادر تو اس پر صدقہ ہے آپ نے فرمایا تجھ کو یہ امر میرے پاس لانے سے پہلے کرنا تھا</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Safwan bin Abdullah bin Safwan se riwayat hai ke Safwan bin Umayya se kisi ne kaha ke jisne hijrat nahi ki toh woh tabah hua, toh Safwan Madina mein aaye aur Masjid-e-Nabvi mein apni chadar sar ke tale rakh kar so rahe. Chor aaya aur chadar unki le gaya. Safwan ne uth kar chor ko giraftar kiya aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas le aaye. Aapne chor se poocha ke kya tu ne Safwan ki chadar churayi? Woh bola haan. Aapne uska haath kaatne ka hukm kiya. Safwan ne kaha meri niyat yeh na thi ya Rasool Allah, woh chadar toh is par sadqa hai. Aapne farmaya tujhko yeh amar mere paas laane se pehle karna tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1529]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1529" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1512

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1530)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1530]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1530" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1530</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1530 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ الزُّبَيْرَ بْنَ الْعَوَّامِ، لَقِيَ رَجُلاً قَدْ أَخَذَ سَارِقًا وَهُوَ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَذْهَبَ بِهِ إِلَى السُّلْطَانِ فَشَفَعَ لَهُ الزُّبَيْرُ لِيُرْسِلَهُ فَقَالَ لاَ حَتَّى أَبْلُغَ بِهِ السُّلْطَانَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الزُّبَيْرُ إِذَا بَلَغْتَ بِهِ السُّلْطَانَ فَلَعَنَ اللَّهُ الشَّافِعَ وَالْمُشَفِّعَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1530" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: az-Zubayr ibn al-Awwam (companion). The source transmits az-Zubayr&#x27;s intercession and warning. Keep the source&#x27;s Companion attribution and do not substitute a Prophetic speaker.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman that az-Zubayr ibn al-Awwam came across a man who had taken hold of a thief and was intending to take him to the Sultan. Az-Zubayr ibn al- Awwam interceded for him to let him go. He said, &quot;No. Not until I take him to the Sultan.&quot; Az-Zubayr said, &quot;When you reach the Sultan with him, Allah curses the one who intercedes and the one who accepts the intercession</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1530]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">रबी&#x27;आह बिन आबि अब्दुल रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के जुबैर बिन एव्वं ने एक शख़्स को देखा के वह चोर को पकडे हुए हाकिम के पास लिए जाता था. ज़ुबैर ने सिफारिश की हाकिम के पास न ले जाऊंगा. ज़ुबैर ने कहा जब तू हाकिम के पास ले गया तोह अल्लाह की लानत सिफारिश करने वाले पर और सिफारिश मानने वाले पर.</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rabi&#x27;ah bin Abi Abdul Rahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Zubair bin Awwam ne ek shakhs ko dekha ke woh chor ko pakde hue hakim ke paas liye jata tha. Zubair ne sifarish ki, kaha chhod de. Woh bola kabhi nahi chhodunga jab tak ke hakim ke paas na le jaunga. Zubair ne kaha jab tu hakim ke paas le gaya toh Allah ki lanat sifarish karne wale par aur sifarish manne wale par.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1530" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1513

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1531)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1531]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1531</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1531 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنْ أَهْلِ الْيَمَنِ أَقْطَعَ الْيَدِ وَالرِّجْلِ قَدِمَ فَنَزَلَ عَلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَشَكَا إِلَيْهِ أَنَّ عَامِلَ الْيَمَنِ قَدْ ظَلَمَهُ فَكَانَ يُصَلِّي مِنَ اللَّيْلِ فَيَقُولُ أَبُو بَكْرٍ وَأَبِيكَ مَا لَيْلُكَ بِلَيْلِ سَارِقٍ ‏.‏ ثُمَّ إِنَّهُمْ فَقَدُوا عِقْدًا لأَسْمَاءَ بِنْتِ عُمَيْسٍ امْرَأَةِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَجَعَلَ الرَّجُلُ يَطُوفُ مَعَهُمْ وَيَقُولُ اللَّهُمَّ عَلَيْكَ بِمَنْ بَيَّتَ أَهْلَ هَذَا الْبَيْتِ الصَّالِحِ ‏.‏ فَوَجَدُوا الْحُلِيَّ عِنْدَ صَائِغٍ زَعَمَ أَنَّ الأَقْطَعَ جَاءَهُ بِهِ فَاعْتَرَفَ بِهِ الأَقْطَعُ أَوْ شُهِدَ عَلَيْهِ بِهِ فَأَمَرَ بِهِ أَبُو بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقُ فَقُطِعَتْ يَدُهُ الْيُسْرَى وَقَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ وَاللَّهِ لَدُعَاؤُهُ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ أَشَدُّ عِنْدِي عَلَيْهِ مِنْ سَرِقَتِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الَّذِي يَسْرِقُ مِرَارًا ثُمَّ يُسْتَعْدَى عَلَيْهِ إِنَّهُ لَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ إِلاَّ أَنْ تُقْطَعَ يَدُهُ لِجَمِيعِ مَنْ سَرَقَ مِنْهُ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ أُقِيمَ عَلَيْهِ الْحَدُّ فَإِنْ كَانَ قَدْ أُقِيمَ عَلَيْهِ الْحَدُّ قَبْلَ ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ سَرَقَ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ قُطِعَ أَيْضًا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Bakr as-Siddiq (companion). Select the narrated theft case and Abu Bakr&#x27;s reported statements. Malik&#x27;s subsequent general discussion of repeated theft remains source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasim from his father that a man from Yemen who had his hand and foot cut off came and went before Abu Bakr as-Siddiq and complained to him that the governor of the Yemen had wronged him, and the man used to pray part of the night. Abu Bakr said, &quot;By your father, your night is not the night of a thief.&quot; Then they missed a necklace of Asma bint Umays, the wife of Abu Bakr as-Siddiq. The man came to go around with them looking for it. He said, &quot;O Allah! You are responsible for the one who invaded the people of this good house by night!&quot; They found the jewelry with a goldsmith. He claimed that the maimed man had brought it to him. The maimed man confessed or it was testified against him. Abu Bakr as-Siddiq ordered that his left hand be cut off. Abu Bakr said, &quot;By Allah! His dua against himself is more serious, as far as I am concerned, than his theft.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1531]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1531</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1531 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;What is done among us about the person who steals several times and is then called to reckoning, is that only his hand is cut off for all he stole when the hadd has not been applied againsthim. If the hadd has been applied against him before that, and he steals what obliges cutting off, then the next limb is cut off</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1531]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1531</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1531 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद से रिवायत है के एक शख़्स यमन का रहने वाला हाथ पैर कटा हुआ मदीना में आया और अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास उतर कर बोला के यमन के हाकिम ने मुझ पर ज़ुल्म किया और वो रातों को नमाज़ पढ़ता था। अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा क़सम तेरे बाप की तेरी रात चोरों की रात नहीं है। इत्तेफ़ाक़न एक हार अस्मा बिंत उमैस, अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) की बीवी का गुम हो गया। लोगों के साथ वो लंगड़ा भी ढूँढता फिरता था और कहता था के ऐ परवरदिगार तबाह कर उसको जिसने ऐसे नेक घर वालों के यहाँ चोरी की। फिर वो हार एक सुनार के पास मिला, सुनार बोला मुझे इस लंगड़े ने दिया है। इस लंगड़े ने इक़रार किया या गवाही से साबित हुआ, हज़रत अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने हुक्म किया तो उसका बायां हाथ काटा गया। हज़रत अबू बक्र (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा क़सम अल्लाह की मुझे इसकी बद-दुआ जो अपने ऊपर करता था चोरी से ज़्यादा सख़्त मालूम हुई। कहा मालिक ने अगर एक शख़्स ने कई बार चोरी की बाद उसके गिरफ़्तार हुआ तो सब चोरियों के बदले में सिर्फ़ उसका हाथ काटा जाएगा जब उसका हाथ न कटा हो और जो हाथ कटने के बाद उसने चोरी की रबा दीनार के मुवाफ़िक़ तो बायां पाँव काटा जाएगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص یمن کا رہنے والا ہاتھ پاؤں کٹا ہوا مدینہ میں آیا اور ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس اتر کر بولا کہ یمن کے حاکم نے مجھ پر ظلم کیا اور وہ راتوں کو نماز پڑھتا تھا ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا کہ قسم تیرے باپ کی تیری رات چوروں کی رات نہیں ہے۔ اتفاقا ایک ہار اسما بنت عمیس ابوبکر کی بی بی کا گم ہو گیا لوگوں کے ساتھ وہ لنگڑا بھی ڈھونڈتا پھرتا تھا اور کہتا تھا کہ اے پروردگار تباہ کر اس کو جس نے ایسے نیک گھر والوں کے ہاں چوری کی، پھر وہ ہار ایک سنار کے پاس ملا سنار بولا مجھے اس لنگڑے نے دیا ہے اس لنگڑے نے اقرار کیا یا گواہی سے ثابت ہوا حضرت ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے حکم کیا تو اس کا بایاں ہاتھ کاٹا گیا حضرت ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا قسم اللہ کی مجھے اس کی بدعا جو اپنے اوپر کرتا تھا چوری سے زیادہ سخت معلوم ہوئی ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر ایک شخص نے کئی بار چوری کی بعد اس کے گرفتار ہوا تو سب چوریوں کے بدلے میں صرف اس کا ہاتھ کاٹا جائے گا جب اس کا ہاتھ نہ کٹاہو اور جو ہاتھ کٹنے کے بعد اس نے چوری کی ربع رینار کے موافق تو بایاں پاؤں کاٹا جائے گا۔</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1531]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1531</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1531 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad se riwayat hai ke ek shakhs Yemen ka rehne wala haath pair kata hua Madina mein aaya aur Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas utar kar bola ke Yemen ke hakim ne mujh par zulm kiya aur woh raaton ko namaz padhta tha. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha qasam tere baap ki teri raat choron ki raat nahi hai. Ittefaaqan ek haar Asma bint Umais, Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ki biwi ka gum ho gaya. Logon ke saath woh langda bhi dhoondta phirta tha aur kehta tha ke aye Parwardigar tabah kar usko jisne aise naik ghar walon ke yahan chori ki. Phir woh haar ek sunaar ke paas mila, sunaar bola mujhe is langde ne diya hai. Is langde ne iqrar kiya ya gawahi se saabit hua, Hazrat Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne hukm kiya toh uska baayan haath kaata gaya. Hazrat Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha qasam Allah ki mujhe iski bad-dua jo apne upar karta tha chori se zyada sakht maloom hui. Kaha Malik ne agar ek shakhs ne kai baar chori ki baad uske giraftar hua toh sab choriyon ke badle mein sirf uska haath kaata jayega jab uska haath na kata ho aur jo haath katne ke baad usne chori ki raba dinar ke muwafiq toh baayan paanv kaata jayega.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1531" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1514

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 41</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1532]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1532" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1532</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1532 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ أَبَا الزِّنَادِ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ عَامِلاً لِعُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ أَخَذَ نَاسًا فِي حِرَابَةٍ وَلَمْ يَقْتُلُوا أَحَدًا فَأَرَادَ أَنْ يَقْطَعَ أَيْدِيَهُمْ أَوْ يَقْتُلَ فَكَتَبَ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ فِي ذَلِكَ فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ لَوْ أَخَذْتَ بِأَيْسَرِ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى وَسَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الَّذِي يَسْرِقُ أَمْتِعَةَ النَّاسِ الَّتِي تَكُونُ مَوْضُوعَةً بِالأَسْوَاقِ مُحْرَزَةً قَدْ أَحْرَزَهَا أَهْلُهَا فِي أَوْعِيَتِهِمْ وَضَمُّوا بَعْضَهَا إِلَى بَعْضٍ إِنَّهُ مَنْ سَرَقَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ شَيْئًا مِنْ حِرْزِهِ فَبَلَغَ قِيمَتُهُ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ فَإِنَّ عَلَيْهِ الْقَطْعَ كَانَ صَاحِبُ الْمَتَاعِ عِنْدَ مَتَاعِهِ أَوْ لَمْ يَكُنْ لَيْلاً ذَلِكَ أَوْ نَهَارًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الَّذِي يَسْرِقُ مَا يَجِبُ عَلَيْهِ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ ثُمَّ يُوجَدُ مَعَهُ مَا سَرَقَ فَيُرَدُّ إِلَى صَاحِبِهِ إِنَّهُ تُقْطَعُ يَدُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَإِنْ قَالَ قَائِلٌ كَيْفَ تُقْطَعُ يَدُهُ وَقَدْ أُخِذَ الْمَتَاعُ مِنْهُ وَدُفِعَ إِلَى صَاحِبِهِ فَإِنَّمَا هُوَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ الشَّارِبِ يُوجَدُ مِنْهُ رِيحُ الشَّرَابِ الْمُسْكِرِ وَلَيْسَ بِهِ سُكْرٌ فَيُجْلَدُ الْحَدَّ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَإِنَّمَا يُجْلَدُ الْحَدَّ فِي الْمُسْكِرِ إِذَا شَرِبَهُ وَإِنْ لَمْ يُسْكِرْهُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا شَرِبَهُ لِيُسْكِرَهُ فَكَذَلِكَ تُقْطَعُ يَدُ السَّارِقِ فِي السَّرِقَةِ الَّتِي أُخِذَتْ مِنْهُ وَلَوْ لَمْ يَنْتَفِعْ بِهَا وَرَجَعَتْ إِلَى صَاحِبِهَا وَإِنَّمَا سَرَقَهَا حِينَ سَرَقَهَا لِيَذْهَبَ بِهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْقَوْمِ يَأْتُونَ إِلَى الْبَيْتِ فَيَسْرِقُونَ مِنْهُ جَمِيعًا فَيَخْرُجُونَ بِالْعِدْلِ يَحْمِلُونَهُ جَمِيعًا أَوِ الصُّنْدُوقِ أَوِ الْخَشَبَةِ أَوْ بِالْمِكْتَلِ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِمَّا يَحْمِلُهُ الْقَوْمُ جَمِيعًا إِنَّهُمْ إِذَا أَخْرَجُوا ذَلِكَ مِنْ حِرْزِهِ وَهُمْ يَحْمِلُونَهُ جَمِيعًا فَبَلَغَ ثَمَنُ مَا خَرَجُوا بِهِ مِنْ ذَلِكَ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ - وَذَلِكَ ثَلاَثَةُ دَرَاهِمَ فَصَاعِدًا - فَعَلَيْهِمُ الْقَطْعُ جَمِيعًا ‏.‏ قَالَ وَإِنْ خَرَجَ كُلُّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمْ بِمَتَاعٍ عَلَى حِدَتِهِ فَمَنْ خَرَجَ مِنْهُمْ بِمَا تَبْلُغُ قِيمَتُهُ ثَلاَثَةَ دَرَاهِمَ فَصَاعِدًا فَعَلَيْهِ الْقَطْعُ وَمَنْ لَمْ يَخْرُجْ مِنْهُمْ بِمَا تَبْلُغُ قِيمَتُهُ ثَلاَثَةَ دَرَاهِمَ فَلاَ قَطْعَ عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ إِذَا كَانَتْ دَارُ رَجُلٍ مُغْلَقَةً عَلَيْهِ لَيْسَ مَعَهُ فِيهَا غَيْرُهُ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَجِبُ عَلَى مَنْ سَرَقَ مِنْهَا شَيْئًا الْقَطْعُ حَتَّى يَخْرُجَ بِهِ مِنَ الدَّارِ كُلِّهَا وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ الدَّارَ كُلَّهَا هِيَ حِرْزُهُ فَإِنْ كَانَ مَعَهُ فِي الدَّارِ سَاكِنٌ غَيْرُهُ وَكَانَ كُلُّ إِنْسَانٍ مِنْهُمْ يُغْلِقُ عَلَيْهِ بَابَهُ وَكَانَتْ حِرْزًا لَهُمْ جَمِيعًا فَمَنْ سَرَقَ مِنْ بُيُوتِ تِلْكَ الدَّارِ شَيْئًا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ فَخَرَجَ بِهِ إِلَى الدَّارِ فَقَدْ أَخْرَجَهُ مِنْ حِرْزِهِ إِلَى غَيْرِ حِرْزِهِ وَوَجَبَ عَلَيْهِ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الْعَبْدِ يَسْرِقُ مِنْ مَتَاعِ سَيِّدِهِ أَنَّهُ إِنْ كَانَ لَيْسَ مِنْ خَدَمِهِ وَلاَ مِمَّنْ يَأْمَنُ عَلَى بَيْتِهِ ثُمَّ دَخَلَ سِرًّا فَسَرَقَ مِنْ مَتَاعِ سَيِّدِهِ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ فَلاَ قَطْعَ عَلَيْهِ وَكَذَلِكَ الأَمَةُ إِذَا سَرَقَتْ مِنْ مَتَاعِ سَيِّدِهَا لاَ قَطْعَ عَلَيْهَا ‏.‏ وَقَالَ فِي الْعَبْدِ لاَ يَكُونُ مِنْ خَدَمِهِ وَلاَ مِمَّنْ يَأْمَنُ عَلَى بَيْتِهِ فَدَخَلَ سِرًّا فَسَرَقَ مِنْ مَتَاعِ امْرَأَةِ سَيِّدِهِ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ إِنَّهُ تُقْطَعُ يَدُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَكَذَلِكَ أَمَةُ الْمَرْأَةِ إِذَا كَانَتْ لَيْسَتْ بِخَادِمٍ لَهَا وَلاَ لِزَوْجِهَا وَلاَ مِمَّنْ تَأْمَنُ عَلَى بَيْتِهَا فَدَخَلَتْ سِرًّا فَسَرَقَتْ مِنْ مَتَاعِ سَيِّدَتِهَا مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ فَلاَ قَطْعَ عَلَيْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَكَذَلِكَ أَمَةُ الْمَرْأَةِ الَّتِي لاَ تَكُونُ مِنْ خَدَمِهَا وَلاَ مِمَّنْ تَأْمَنُ عَلَى بَيْتِهَا فَدَخَلَتْ سِرًّا فَسَرَقَتْ مِنْ مَتَاعِ زَوْجِ سَيِّدَتِهَا مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ أَنَّهَا تُقْطَعُ يَدُهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَكَذَلِكَ الرَّجُلُ يَسْرِقُ مِنْ مَتَاعِ امْرَأَتِهِ أَوِ الْمَرْأَةُ تَسْرِقُ مِنْ مَتَاعِ زَوْجِهَا مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ إِنْ كَانَ الَّذِي سَرَقَ كُلُّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمَا مِنْ مَتَاعِ صَاحِبِهِ فِي بَيْتٍ سِوَى الْبَيْتِ الَّذِي يُغْلِقَانِ عَلَيْهِمَا وَكَانَ فِي حِرْزٍ سِوَى الْبَيْتِ الَّذِي هُمَا فِيهِ فَإِنَّ مَنْ سَرَقَ مِنْهُمَا مِنْ مَتَاعِ صَاحِبِهِ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ فَعَلَيْهِ الْقَطْعُ فِيهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الصَّبِيِّ الصَّغِيرِ وَالأَعْجَمِيِّ الَّذِي لاَ يُفْصِحُ أَنَّهُمَا إِذَا سُرِقَا مِنْ حِرْزِهِمَا أَوْ غَلْقِهِمَا فَعَلَى مَنْ سَرَقَهُمَا الْقَطْعُ وَإِنْ خَرَجَا مِنْ حِرْزِهِمَا وَغَلْقِهِمَا فَلَيْسَ عَلَى مَنْ سَرَقَهُمَا قَطْعٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَإِنَّمَا هُمَا بِمَنْزِلَةِ حَرِيسَةِ الْجَبَلِ وَالثَّمَرِ الْمُعَلَّقِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الَّذِي يَنْبِشُ الْقُبُورَ أَنَّهُ إِذَا بَلَغَ مَا أَخْرَجَ مِنَ الْقَبْرِ مَا يَجِبُ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ فَعَلَيْهِ فِيهِ الْقَطْعُ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ مَالِكٌ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ الْقَبْرَ حِرْزٌ لِمَا فِيهِ كَمَا أَنَّ الْبُيُوتَ حِرْزٌ لِمَا فِيهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ وَلاَ يَجِبُ عَلَيْهِ الْقَطْعُ حَتَّى يَخْرُجَ بِهِ مِنَ الْقَبْرِ ‏.‏</p>
